- **tacit.knowledge**: Query the local tacit knowledge database (~/.tacit/) for relevant context. TRIGGER when: user asks about topics that might have been previously captured — past conversations, meetings, decisions, ideas, learnings, or any domain knowledge. DO NOT TRIGGER when: purely code-editing tasks, git operations, or questions clearly unrelated to stored knowledge. ([sangmin7648/tacit](https://github.com/sangmin7648/tacit/tree/HEAD/tacit/core/internal/components/skill-installer/tacit.knowledge) · ★11)

TRIGGER when: the user invokes /tacit.memorize, wants to save the current conversation to memory, or asks to record what was discussed. DO NOT TRIGGER for: querying existing knowledge (use /tacit.knowledge instead).
